# Seed 53 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small (Iteration 320)

`seed 53 tag2 3h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 3 --frontier --cpus 2,8`, commit
d578d6279 (Iteration 319) - the first run with the after-seed-52 batch (the JIT fix, the gene
bfind), the first through the new runner: one chain, one archive (chain-20261016-003022), with
the remote checks alongside. The chain 00:30-02:59 UTC Oct 16 (2 h 29 min): the evolution
00:39-01:59 (1 h 20 min), the re-measure 16 min, the comparison at 3 rounds 44 min. 7,665
designs, 6,131 measured, 1,507 screened.

The comparison's verdict (five measures, 3 rounds - noisier than seed 52's 6): fastest -5.8%,
smallest +0.00%, memory -4 KB, hypervolume +0.3%, best Q -5.3% - **small**. The Q-best design of
all runs: seed 52's c0fa7633d0, 0.145 at 28,157 bytes, 168 KB (Q 0.211 of s6's).

- **No corpus deaths** - 27 refused before running, none wrong (seed 52: 2, the JIT fault).
- bfind on 17 of the 172 designs of the front, after 80 minutes; the JIT on 96, jitloop with it on all.
- Children: mutation 56.7%, crossover 31.3% (the last fifth 30.2%), borrowed 12.0%.
- **The remote checks** (results/xcheck-dream-seed53.md): dream.lan (i7-6700, Skylake), 24 designs
  of seed 52's front, the laptop's binaries - Spearman 0.959, the laptop's six fastest its first
  six; the JIT designs' lead over s6 smaller there (0.128 of s6's time on the laptop, 0.223 on
  dream). rage.lan: no results - build-stages.sh failed there, a hand-made stage with the gene
  sysc, whose system-call numbers are x86-64's (relf.c refuses it on ARM).
- Held out (heldout.py):
  sieve (held out): 172 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.948
    the 10 fastest by selection, their rank on it: 3 4 1 2 10 7 11 8 5 6
    held-out / selection speed: min 0.39, median 0.61, max 2.84
    flattered most by the selection: 8ab9fcf0cc 2.84, 4c983316e2 2.71, 550ceafb91 2.67, a173d54720 2.66, 1b5e4babc7 2.62

---

# Evolved VM designs

6158 designs evaluated, 6131 alive, 1507 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 04edc97281, speed 0.138, binary + image 28,157, memory 168 KB - Q 0.199 of s6-cv8b's.
